Model before rewriting:

letting n be 4
letting mycon be {{(1,0),(4,0)},{(2,0),(4,0)},{(3,0),(4,0)},{(1,1),(2,1),(3,1),(4,1)}}

shortTable(sequence(x[1],x[2],x[3],x[4]), mycon)
